000082_c2f9d302-974b-a0e0-0d92-44e06db1c8ccSTANDOC_48
(generic warning) Introduction may contain requirement: The objective is to provide a neutral mechanism capable of describing products throughout their life cycle
<introduction id="_c2f9d302-974b-a0e0-0d92-44e06db1c8cc" obligation="informative">
<title id="_2b2e98d1-114a-3da4-8556-01ae0a724280">Introduction</title>
<p id="_066e9989-6d9a-1f0f-d1fd-73ceba593f3d">ISO 10303 is an International Standard for the computer-interpretable representation of product information and for the exchange of product data. The objective is to provide a neutral mechanism capable of describing products throughout their life cycle. This mechanism is suitable not only for neutral file exchange, but also as a basis for implementing and sharing product databases, and as a basis for retention and archiving.</p>

<p id="_6a749f37-1c39-b073-2cba-713d8790fb25">Major subdivisions of this part of ISO 10303 are:</p>

001923approval_​schema.​acyclic_​approval_​relationshipISO_43
subclause is only child
<clause id="_9eca4afb-ca47-8271-a144-fd5489ed1e64" anchor="approval_schema.acyclic_approval_relationship" type="express" inline-header="false" obligation="normative">
<title id="_2316e3db-3cdc-8410-2607-7f349e145c3d">acyclic_approval_relationship <index> <primary>acyclic_approval_relationship</primary> <secondary>Object definition</secondary> </index> </title>
<p id="_18b9b4b9-18af-36a5-a427-ca6815beea14">The <strong>acyclic_approval_relationship</strong> function determines whether the graph of instances of the entity data type <xref target="approval_schema.approval"> <display-text>approval</display-text> </xref> that contains relation as one of its links contains a cycle. This function may be used to evaluate either a <xref target="approval_schema.approval_relationship"> <display-text>approval_relationship</display-text> </xref> or any of its subtypes. </p>

<p id="_d3214a69-fc6f-63f5-8788-1e5185de85f5">The function returns TRUE if no cycle has been detected. Otherwise it returns FALSE.<note id="_8472d51c-8187-fe70-a002-2c71058c8500"> <p id="_572783de-71c0-e064-14b9-85e89f960e67">The algorithm of the function is explained in annex E.2.</p>

002459_39673688-1865-04f5-5e58-3cc6f5347dbeSTANDOC_48
(generic warning) may contain ambiguous provision: The presence of the material certificate removes the need to test the composition of the material;​ it allows the specified material composition to be accepted as fact without further investigation.
<p id="_39673688-1865-04f5-5e58-3cc6f5347dbe">A material certificate states the chemical composition of one or more physical pieces of material, for example, purchased raw material. The presence of the material certificate removes the need to test the composition of the material; it allows the specified material composition to be accepted as fact without further investigation.</p>

<p id="_e405a3e3-a47f-266d-c2f0-01905af88963">The <strong>product_or_formation_or_definition</strong> type is a list of alternate data types. It provides a mechanism to refer to an instance of one of these data types.</p>

<p id="_a794c35f-5aff-95e4-0f82-ef1628283adb">The <strong>product_or_formation_or_definition</strong> type allows for the designation of a <xref target="product_definition_schema.product"> <display-text>product</display-text> </xref>, a <xref target="product_definition_schema.product_definition"> <display-text>product_definition</display-text> </xref>, or a <xref target="product_definition_schema.product_definition_formation"> <display-text>product_definition_formation</display-text> </xref>.</p>
2

<note id="_54393e14-29b5-239c-9867-0bba08efcd73"> <p id="_dcb2cdfc-e58d-5ef6-7564-14934f1ca1ef">This part of ISO 10303 provides several resources to describe documents and to associate them with product data. Annex F.2 provides an explanation about how to describe documents if they are to be considered products.</p>

<example id="_d5313684-aa97-5467-ed4e-12b1fe4277ab"> <p id="_a7e12f02-cf72-4d94-667c-8d658e5c7a2d">For references to a ‘surface finish’ source the <strong>subject_element</strong> may be a ‘Clause 2.1 surface imperfection’ and its associated <strong>subject_element_value</strong> may be ‘no visible imperfections’, ‘no more than two imperfections that are more than 0.06 inch diameter within any given square inch of surface area’, or ‘no imperfections under 10 times magnification’.</p>

004166_acadc177-8ec4-6e46-6684-356a297a59ceSTANDOC_48
(generic warning) Example may contain permission: A surface finish document may be identified by various classes, class A, class B, and class C
<example id="_acadc177-8ec4-6e46-6684-356a297a59ce"> <p id="_a1ce3fe2-648f-c617-8334-f2d4fe4fd352">A surface finish document may be identified by various classes, class A, class B, and class C. Each class of surface finish specifies various allowances for imperfections in the surface finish. Class A may require no visible imperfections, class B may require no more than two imperfections that are more than 0.06 inch diameter within any given square inch of surface area, and class C may require no imperfections under 10 times magnification.</p>
</example>

004191document_​schema.​acyclic_​document_​relationshipISO_43
subclause is only child
<clause id="_bc104b9d-ca26-f6c8-a609-13b5e6d5282c" anchor="document_schema.acyclic_document_relationship" type="express" inline-header="false" obligation="normative">
<title id="_d177d17d-669a-d36c-b80d-bafeed078436">acyclic_document_relationship <index> <primary>acyclic_document_relationship</primary> <secondary>Object definition</secondary> </index> </title>
<p id="_fda784d6-2dc1-d4f9-067c-6cc30eb52766">The <strong>acyclic_document_relationship</strong> function determines whether the graph of instances of the entity data type <xref target="document_schema.document"> <display-text>document</display-text> </xref> that contains relation as one of its links contains a cycle. This function may be used to evaluate either a <xref target="document_schema.document_relationship"> <display-text>document_relationship</display-text> </xref> or any of its subtypes. </p>

<p id="_e80b4e26-bd3c-46b4-299f-c27899d8b997">The function returns TRUE if no cycle has been detected. Otherwise it returns FALSE.<note id="_51020720-c54a-a29f-662f-677e0afde1d7"> <p id="_ef4dfb45-f5fd-8536-b14d-0d4023defa5b">The algorithm of the function is explained in annex E.2.</p>

<p id="_2ea32592-3aee-e119-6f7b-7279482551f5">A <strong>language</strong> is a type of <xref target="group_schema.group"> <display-text>group</display-text> </xref> that is a specification of the language in which an information is given. The group shall specify in its name a language code according to ISO 639-2, and, if present, in its description a country code according to ISO 3166-1.</p>

<p id="_0773067c-3579-aa6a-351f-14d8622b2fad">Informal proposition</p>

005697location_​schema.​acyclic_​location_​relationshipISO_43
subclause is only child
<clause id="_4f9c0aea-5150-d8fa-f705-2c968046f481" anchor="location_schema.acyclic_location_relationship" type="express" inline-header="false" obligation="normative">
<title id="_4dde86a7-9323-8cd9-f56c-d62780b0fd8d">acyclic_location_relationship <index> <primary>acyclic_location_relationship</primary> <secondary>Object definition</secondary> </index> </title>
<p id="_9b6d513c-cc44-e614-3045-ad703d143d0b">The <strong>acyclic_location_relationship</strong> function determines whether the graph of instances of the entity data type <xref target="location_schema.location"> <display-text>location</display-text> </xref> that contains relation as one of its links contains a cycle. This function may be used to evaluate either a <xref target="location_schema.location_relationship"> <display-text>location_relationship</display-text> </xref> or any of its subtypes. </p>

<p id="_8d3967cf-fed2-c6cb-4d24-4efe95d065ce">The function returns TRUE if no cycle has been detected. Otherwise it returns FALSE.<note id="_52a26827-0044-9c38-0ccc-fe311e293681"> <p id="_3f3aeb64-4ab1-77e5-7062-79a0bb956cd6">The algorithm of the function is explained in annex E.2.</p>

012098_ed2e1ada-2854-f6c1-4e95-d77c1b74cbe2STANDOC_48
(generic warning) may contain ambiguous provision: This schema could be used to define the ball-point pen with a solid cap as one product and the one with a hole in the cap as another
<p id="_ed2e1ada-2854-f6c1-4e95-d77c1b74cbe2">An application context may be the manufacture of ball-point pens. A particular design of ball-point pen has multiple versions. Each version is described through a group of product definitions. An older version has a solid cap whereas a more recent version has a hole in the cap to prevent people from choking if they swallow it. The characteristics of the two versions would be different from each other because the later version would have a hole in the cap whereas the earlier version would not. This schema could be used to define the ball-point pen with a solid cap as one product and the one with a hole in the cap as another. On the other hand it could also be used to define both pens as two versions of a single product. The approach taken would depend upon the requirements of the application context.</p>
2

<note id="_fe770b48-581d-1a4d-fc36-83791005422b"> <p id="_432f2934-9ccf-3e2b-ed2a-e5b7fe89a7a0">If a <xref target="product_definition_schema.product_definition_relationship"> <display-text>product_definition_relationship</display-text> </xref> exists between a component part and its assembly, a <strong>product_definition_substitute</strong> could be used to capture an allowable substitute, that is, a different component part that may be used as a replacement in the same assembly.</p>

012859_f8dd95f8-d41b-b6e1-c847-9549dbdcb8f3STANDOC_48
(generic warning) Example may contain permission: Two kinds of ball-point pens may be manufactured: a standard model and a deluxe model
<example id="_f8dd95f8-d41b-b6e1-c847-9549dbdcb8f3"> <p id="_f6d92968-6cb5-8218-d7a7-b1f338a7c313">Two kinds of ball-point pens may be manufactured: a standard model and a deluxe model. Each model of the pen would be specified as a separate <xref target="product_definition_schema.product_definition"> <display-text>product_definition</display-text> </xref> related to a particular kind of nib: a standard nib and a deluxe nib, respectively. The fact that the deluxe nib and the standard nib are interchangeable only in the context of the standard model of ball-point pen is established with this construct.</p>
</example>

<p id="_e797f383-d5bf-a695-2014-6f2e3413354c">A <strong>shape_aspect_relationship</strong> is a relationship between two instances of the entity data type <xref target="product_property_definition_schema.shape_aspect"> <display-text>shape_aspect</display-text> </xref> and provides an identification and description of their relationship. <note id="_ca9cd638-6159-a047-b930-f8cefbd49391"> <p id="_47926e15-9ff5-14de-34b2-9ee625a90126">The role of <strong>shape_aspect_relationship</strong> can be defined in the annotated EXPRESS schemas that use or specialize this entity, or by default, in an agreement of common understanding between the partners sharing this information.</p>
</note> <note id="_6331a687-59ba-0c04-13b2-997843daca1b"> <p id="_d77a2fd2-2614-3ca2-35fb-d8a77f037ece">If one <xref target="product_property_definition_schema.shape_aspect"> <display-text>shape_aspect</display-text> </xref> is part of another, this entity could be used to associate the two <xref target="product_property_definition_schema.shape_aspect"> <display-text>shape_aspect</display-text> </xref>s.</p>

</note> <note id="_7575c152-678f-0349-0952-ec265b1e2591"> <p id="_cba26ed8-f900-5134-35ea-69b6e35fb6fc">No actual physical relationship is established between related instances of the entity data type <xref target="product_property_definition_schema.shape_aspect"> <display-text>shape_aspect</display-text> </xref>.</p>
</note> </p>

<title id="_99bd92d0-2885-51b4-5003-77799e4418a4">Generic product description resource structure</title>
<p id="_27738808-d664-6733-80a5-4530c4587a1f">The generic product description resource is made up of schemas that reflect the different kinds of data that may be associated with a product.</p>

016390_40fceea8-ff8f-bdbe-374a-2dfd3eb2fe84ISO_43
subclause is only child
<clause id="_40fceea8-ff8f-bdbe-374a-2dfd3eb2fe84" inline-header="false" obligation="informative">
<title id="_bf563721-b83e-8416-dd45-11c9ba960e51">acyclic_object_relationship</title>
<p id="_e3f1158b-abde-bb1d-a0ff-9fcf68c1e010">The template described in this clause is used in various ISO 10303 integrated resources. It is a function that detects cyclic definitions.</p>

<p id="_f001dde9-34ac-3ade-2057-c5e49d259001">The <strong>acyclic_object_relationship</strong> function determines whether the graph of instances of the entity data type object that contains relation as one of its links contains a cycle. This function may be used to evaluate either a  <strong>object_relationship</strong> or any of its subtypes.</p>
2

016576_93d2d84d-fff6-e99b-5917-733908312415ISO_21

<title id="_e9f1ddf2-d69c-43aa-ee6d-174b529b1c69">Examples</title>
<clause id="_38733eb5-cae1-6ddf-88a5-a0a92e43ee5a" inline-header="false" obligation="informative">
<title id="_22d81e48-075e-351a-d8b0-5f49b622a32d">Use of the product_definition_schema</title>
<p id="_e63dd0f5-768b-437e-3763-b804ac4d73b8">This clause provides two examples of the use of the entity data type <strong>product_definition</strong> to characterize a version of a product.</p>
1



016613_d7fa65c9-127a-d2b4-b1bd-a0d69d4b655bSTANDOC_48
(generic warning) Hanging paragraph in clause
<clause id="_d7fa65c9-127a-d2b4-b1bd-a0d69d4b655b" inline-header="false" obligation="informative">
<title id="_9b99cef1-5fdb-241e-e688-620157b37585">Document as product</title>
<p id="_b94ef7ca-f3c8-6648-a0ed-15f82a7a97d2">This part of ISO 100303 provides resources that support the association of a reference to an external document with any product data. This association uses the entity data types  <strong>document_reference</strong> and <strong>document</strong>.</p>

<p id="_781bd55f-19cf-f2f8-dded-6aff3b96398e">This part of ISO 10303 also provides resources that support the representation of a document in the case it is managed within the information system as a particular case of product.</p>
2



016620_b8f146d2-5684-dd01-0487-7b692c8fb9a9STANDOC_48
(generic warning) Note may contain permission: Considering a document as a kind of product is a decision that may depend on the application domain and on the emphasis in this domain that is made on the documents versus that made on other kinds of parts
<note id="_b8f146d2-5684-dd01-0487-7b692c8fb9a9"> <p id="_7a2e64ee-8421-9d84-cae4-0952243b3227">Considering a document as a kind of product is a decision that may depend on the application domain and on the emphasis in this domain that is made on the documents versus that made on other kinds of parts. In mechanical products, documents such as operator’s manuals are usually listed in the Bill of Materials of the products to be manufactured and delivered. In such a case, these documents may be tracked in the information systems as particular products.</p>
</note>
2



016664_417b7196-2038-d8f5-aa6f-8dfcc535f379STANDOC_48
(generic warning) may contain ambiguous provision: An example of where a definition might be non-digital might be a case where the physical aspects of a book are relevant.​
<p id="_417b7196-2038-d8f5-aa6f-8dfcc535f379">An example of where a definition might be non-digital might be a case where the physical aspects of a book are relevant.</p>
2

</example>
2



016670_27822543-4653-a72f-0880-c7c6646198e4STANDOC_48
(generic warning) may contain ambiguous provision: If a document is considered as a kind of product and if its composition needs to be represented, the resources provided in ISO 10303-44 to represent assembly structures should be used.
<p id="_27822543-4653-a72f-0880-c7c6646198e4">If a document is considered as a kind of product and if its composition needs to be represented, the resources provided in ISO 10303-44 to represent assembly structures should be used.</p>
2



016673_02539ec6-33d2-0bd1-ab0c-2e17dde51bcaSTANDOC_48
(generic warning) Note may contain permission: For example, the driver’s manual of a car may be identified as a constituent of the car using an instance of next_​assembly_​usage_​occurrence that relates two instances of the entity data type product_​definition,​ identifying definitions of the driver’s manual and of the car respectively
<note id="_02539ec6-33d2-0bd1-ab0c-2e17dde51bca"> <p id="_3cfbf6f5-78d9-ac47-7f2c-c90bd1c02ad3">The entity data type <strong>next_assembly_usage_occurrence</strong> can also be used to represent the fact that a document is a constituent of any other kind of product. For example, the driver’s manual of a car may be identified as a constituent of the car using an instance of <strong>next_assembly_usage_occurrence</strong> that relates two instances of the entity data type <strong>product_definition</strong>, identifying definitions of the driver’s manual and of the car respectively.</p>
</note>
2



016685_d29c223d-5a6c-42e7-d958-e2e2cf1ac4bbSTANDOC_48
(generic warning) Note may contain recommendation: Consequently, in such a case, the association of document data with product data should not be dealt with using the entity data types product_​definition_​relationship, product_​definition_​formation_​relationsh­ip or product_​relationshi­p
<note id="_d29c223d-5a6c-42e7-d958-e2e2cf1ac4bb"> <p id="_a6e54053-880c-e3d5-99be-3666b65c5f05">Consequently, in such a case, the association of document data with product data should not be dealt with using the entity data types  <strong>product_definition_relationship</strong>, <strong>product_definition_formation_relationship</strong> or <strong>product_relationship</strong>.</p>
</note>
2



016693_0b110eca-a1f8-c23a-53ee-53b516c09235STANDOC_48
(generic warning) may contain ambiguous provision: In order to enable the use of the document specific resources, as listed above, for a document considered as a kind of product, one needs to relate an instance of the entity data type document with an instance corresponding either to the identification of the document,​ to a version of the document or to a definition of the document
<p id="_0b110eca-a1f8-c23a-53ee-53b516c09235">In order to enable the use of the document specific resources, as listed above, for a document considered as a kind of product, one needs to relate an instance of the entity data type  <strong>document</strong> with an instance corresponding either to the identification of the document, to a version of the document or to a definition of the document. This association can be done using the entity data type  <strong>document_product_association</strong>. For example, in order to state that an instance of the entity data type  <strong>document</strong> identifies the same collection of information as a version of the corresponding product, an instance of <strong>document_product_association</strong> will relate the instance of document and the corresponding instance of <strong>product_definition_formation</strong>. The attribute document_product_association.name will have the value ‘equivalence’.</p>
2

<title id="_6e23fb5e-23a8-77ef-a20f-dca990513850">SI derived units</title>
<p id="_773161c0-a172-b499-f7fb-e9b5395cfc62">SI derived unit exchange should use the <strong>derived_unit</strong> and <strong>unit_elements</strong> referencing either a SI base unit or other SI derived units rather
than relying directly on <strong>dimensional_exponents</strong>.<note id="_3d405421-531e-0152-6685-b6e70680ac75"> <p id="_077ca29f-9450-11de-4e67-a8c286fccb47">This part of ISO 10303 addresses the kilogram issue by isolating the application of .KILO. used to define a unit from the application of .KILO. used as a prefix in a measure value.</p>
</note> </p>

named ‘square millimetre’.<note id="_dc3c063d-1b20-c14d-d284-30c08544cae7"> <p id="_e5584b0c-fd15-4390-640f-7254da8903fc">The instances are expressed using the notation of ISO 10303-21 [2].</p>

019065_4640f9b0-ba65-28bd-2085-6bb4a59f0906ISO_41
